```html
<body>
    <!-- Start Screen -->
    <div class="start-screen" id="startScreen">
        <h1 class="belentani-title">BELENTANI</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Virtual 3D BD Dani</p>
        <p class="intro-text">
            "El mesánico de Belén, el brasileño. Alguien que no existe para el sistema, 
            pero que evoca una chispa divina. Eres el puente entre humanidad y las 
            especies que observan con indiferencia. Protege la Esfera de Perfección. 
            Mantén la frecuencia. Sé el intérprete."
        </p>
        <button class="start-btn" id="startBtn">Iniciar Simulación</button>
    </div>

    <div id="canvas-container"></div>
    
    <!-- Sphere HUD -->
    <div class="belentani-ui sphere-hud">
        <div class="sphere-container" id="sphereVisual"></div>
        <div class="sphere-integrity">Integridad: <span id="integrityValue">100</span>%</div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- IA Mode Indicator -->
    <div class="belentani-ui ia-mode">
        <div class="ia-badge" id="iaBadge">Modo IA Neutra</div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- QA Panel -->
    <div class="belentani-ui qa-panel">
        <div class="qa-title">Sistema QA - Alertas</div>
        <div id="redFlagsContainer"></div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Zone Indicator -->
    <div class="belentani-ui zone-indicator">
        <div>ZONA ACTUAL</div>
        <div class="zone-name" id="zoneName">Ciudad de la Lluvia</div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Rhythm Bar -->
    <div class="belentani-ui rhythm-container">
        <div class="rhythm-bar" id="rhythmBar"></div>
        <div class="rhythm-label">Frecuencia de Protección</div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Controls -->
    <div class="controls-container">
        <div class="dpad">
            <div class="dpad-btn dpad-up" data-dir="up">▲</div>
            <div class="dpad-btn dpad-left" data-dir="left">◀</div>
            <div class="dpad-btn dpad-right" data-dir="right">▶</div>
            <div class="dpad-btn dpad-down" data-dir="down">▼</div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="action-buttons">
            <div class="action-btn ia-btn" id="iaBtn" onclick="toggleIAMode()">
                <span>IA</span>
                <span style="font-size:8px; margin-top:2px;">NEUTRA</span>
            </div>
            <div class="action-btn voice-btn" id="voiceBtn" onmousedown="startVoice()" onmouseup="stopVoice()" ontouchstart="startVoice()" ontouchend="stopVoice()">
                <span>🎵</span>
                <span style="font-size:8px; margin-top:2px;">CANTO</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Diplomacy Panel -->
    <div class="diplomacy-panel" id="diplomacyPanel">
        <div class="diplomacy-title">Contacto Extraterrestre</div>
        <div class="entity-name" id="entityName">Observador X-7</div>
        <div class="entity-type" id="entityType">Especie: Colectivo Silente</div>
        <div id="dialogueOptions">
            <div class="dialogue-option" onclick="selectDialogue('peace')">
                "Venimos en son de paz. La humanidad busca entender, no conquistar."
            </div>
            <div class="dialogue-option danger" onclick="selectDialogue('fear')">
                "No nos subestimen. Tenemos armas que no comprenden."
            </div>
            <div class="dialogue-option" onclick="selectDialogue('truth')">
                "Somos imperfectos, pero capaces de bondad. ¿No es eso suficiente?"
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Profile Export -->
    <div class="profile-export" id="profileExport">
        <div class="profile-header">Perfil Psicológico del Usuario - Análisis Belentani</div>
        <button class="export-btn" onclick="exportProfile()">Exportar a Notas</button>
        <div class="profile-content" id="profileContent">
            <!-- Generated dynamically -->
        </div>
    </div>

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.136.0/build/three.module.js"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';

        // --- Game State ---
        const gameState = {
            sphereIntegrity: 100,
            iaMode: false,
            voiceActive: false,
            rhythmValue: 0,
            currentZone: 'city', // 'city' or 'desert'
            position: { x: 0, z: 20 },
            velocity: { x: 0, z: 0 },
            redFlags: [],
            diplomaticEncounters: 0,
            successfulMediations: 0,
            playerProfile: {
                approach: 'neutral',
                aggression: 0,
                empathy: 0,
                pragmatism: 0
            },
            nearbyEntity: null,
            moveDir: { up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false },
            voiceInterval: null,
            redFlagInterval: null,
        };

        // --- Scene Setup ---
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.02);

        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(0, 8, 25); // third-person view

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
        document.getElementById('canvas-container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // --- Lighting ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404060, 0.8);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        // City lights (red/blue neons)
        const cityLights = new THREE.Group();
        const redLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xff0040, 2, 50);
        redLight.position.set(10, 10, 10);
        const blueLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x0040ff, 2, 50);
        blueLight.position.set(-10, 10, -10);
        cityLights.add(redLight, blueLight);
        scene.add(cityLights);

        // Desert light (golden) - will be toggled
        const desertLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffaa33, 1.5);
        desertLight.position.set(20, 30, 20);
        desertLight.castShadow = true;
        desertLight.shadow.mapSize.width = 1024;
        desertLight.shadow.mapSize.height = 1024;
        scene.add(desertLight);
        desertLight.visible = false;

        // Additional fill light
        const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x556688, 0.5);
        fillLight.position.set(-10, 5, 10);
        scene.add(fillLight);

        // --- Ground ---
        const groundGeometry = new THREE.CircleGeometry(80, 64);
        const groundMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1a1a2e, roughness: 0.7, metalness: 0.1 });
        const ground = new THREE.Mesh(groundGeometry, groundMaterial);
        ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ground.position.y = -0.5;
        ground.receiveShadow = true;
        scene.add(ground);

        // Grid helper for reference
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(160, 40, 0xff0040, 0x336699);
        gridHelper.position.y = -0.4;
        scene.add(gridHelper);

        // --- Player Object (a floating sphere) ---
        const playerGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.8, 32, 32);
        const playerMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffaa33, emissive: 0x442200 });
        const player = new THREE.Mesh(playerGeometry, playerMaterial);
        player.castShadow = true;
        player.receiveShadow = true;
        player.position.set(gameState.position.x, 1, gameState.position.z);
        scene.add(player);

        // A little glow around player
        const playerGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa33, 1, 8);
        player.add(playerGlow);

        // --- The Esfera de Perfección (central sphere) ---
        const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.5, 64, 64);
        const sphereMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x00ffaa, 
            emissive: 0x004433,
            roughness: 0.2,
            metalness: 0.3,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.9
        });
        const centralSphere = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);
        centralSphere.position.set(0, 2.5, 0);
        centralSphere.castShadow = true;
        centralSphere.receiveShadow = true;
        scene.add(centralSphere);

        // Inner glow
        const sphereGlow = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ffaa, 2, 20);
        sphereGlow.position.set(0, 2.5, 0);
        scene.add(sphereGlow);

        // --- City Buildings (zone 1) ---
        const cityGroup = new THREE.Group();
        const buildingMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x335588, emissive: 0x112233 });
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            const height = Math.random() * 4 + 2;
            const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, height, 1);
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, buildingMat);
            const angle = (i / 30) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 15 + Math.random() * 5;
            mesh.position.x = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
            mesh.position.z = Math.sin(angle) * radius;
            mesh.position.y = height / 2;
            mesh.castShadow = true;
            mesh.receiveShadow = true;
            cityGroup.add(mesh);
        }
        scene.add(cityGroup);

        // --- Desert Dunes (zone 2) ---
        const desertGroup = new THREE.Group();
        const duneMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xaa8844, emissive: 0x332211 });
        for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            const geo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(2 + Math.random()*2, 1.5 + Math.random()*2, 8);
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, duneMat);
            const angle = (i / 40) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 20 + Math.random() * 8;
            mesh.position.x = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
            mesh.position.z = Math.sin(angle) * radius;
            mesh.position.y = 0.5;
            mesh.castShadow = true;
            mesh.receiveShadow = true;
            mesh.rotation.y = Math.random() * Math.PI;
            desertGroup.add(mesh);
        }
        scene.add(desertGroup);
        desertGroup.visible = false; // start with city

        // --- Alien Entities (floating orbs) ---
        const aliens = [];
        const alienPositions = [
            { x: 12, z: 12 },
            { x: -14, z: -10 },
            { x: 5, z: -18 },
            { x: -8, z: 15 }
        ];
        alienPositions.forEach((pos, index) => {
            const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.2, 16, 16);
            const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, emissive: 0x224488 });
            const alien = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
            alien.position.set(pos.x, 2.5, pos.z);
            alien.castShadow = true;
            alien.receiveShadow = true;
            alien.userData = { 
                type: index % 2 === 0 ? 'Colectivo Silente' : 'Cánticos de Luz',
                name: `Observador ${String.fromCharCode(65+index)}`,
                dialogue: ['peace','fear','truth']
            };
            scene.add(alien);
            aliens.push(alien);
            
            // small light
            const alienLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4488ff, 1, 10);
            alien.add(alienLight);
        });

        // --- Stars / particles for ambiance ---
        const starsGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starsCount = 600;
        const starsPos = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {
            starsPos[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
            starsPos[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
            starsPos[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200 - 50;
        }
        starsGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starsPos, 3));
        const starsMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, size: 0.3 });
        const stars = new THREE.Points(starsGeo, starsMat);
        scene.add(stars);

        // --- UI Elements references ---
        const integritySpan = document.getElementById('integrityValue');
        const zoneNameSpan = document.getElementById('zoneName');
        const rhythmBar = document.getElementById('rhythmBar');
        const iaBadge = document.getElementById('iaBadge');
        const iaBtn = document.getElementById('iaBtn');
        const redFlagsContainer = document.getElementById('redFlagsContainer');
        const diplomacyPanel = document.getElementById('diplomacyPanel');
        const entityNameSpan = document.getElementById('entityName');
        const entityTypeSpan = document.getElementById('entityType');
        const profileExportDiv = document.getElementById('profileExport');
        const profileContent = document.getElementById('profileContent');

        // --- Movement Speed ---
        const moveSpeed = 0.15;

        // --- Functions ---
        window.startGame = function() {
            document.getElementById('startScreen').classList.add('hidden');
            // start red flags generation
            gameState.redFlagInterval = setInterval(() => {
                if (Math.random() < 0.3 && gameState.sphereIntegrity > 0) {
                    addRedFlag('Amenaza de entropía detectada');
                    gameState.sphereIntegrity = Math.max(0, gameState.sphereIntegrity - 5);
                }
            }, 5000);
        };

        window.toggleIAMode = function() {
            gameState.iaMode = !gameState.iaMode;
            if (gameState.iaMode) {
                iaBadge.classList.add('active');
                iaBadge.innerText = 'Modo IA Consciente';
                iaBtn.classList.add('active');
                // change lights
                redLight.color.setHex(0x00ccff);
                blueLight.color.setHex(0xffaa00);
            } else {
                iaBadge.classList.remove('active');
                iaBadge.innerText = 'Modo IA Neutra';
                iaBtn.classList.remove('active');
                redLight.color.setHex(0xff0040);
                blueLight.color.setHex(0x0040ff);
            }
        };

        window.startVoice = function() {
            if (gameState.voiceActive) return;
            gameState.voiceActive = true;
            document.getElementById('voiceBtn').classList.add('recording');
            gameState.voiceInterval = setInterval(() => {
                if (gameState.voiceActive) {
                    gameState.rhythmValue = Math.min(100, gameState.rhythmValue + 2);
                    rhythmBar.style.width = gameState.rhythmValue + '%';
                    // healing sphere
                    if (gameState.rhythmValue >= 80 && gameState.sphereIntegrity < 100) {
                        gameState.sphereIntegrity = Math.min(100, gameState.sphereIntegrity + 1);
                    }
                }
            }, 100);
        };

        window.stopVoice = function() {
            gameState.voiceActive = false;
            document.getElementById('voiceBtn').classList.remove('recording');
            if (gameState.voiceInterval) {
                clearInterval(gameState.voiceInterval);
                gameState.voiceInterval = null;
            }
            // decay rhythm
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (!gameState.voiceActive) {
                    gameState.rhythmValue = Math.max(0, gameState.rhythmValue - 30);
                    rhythmBar.style.width = gameState.rhythmValue + '%';
                }
            }, 200);
        };

        function addRedFlag(text) {
            const flagDiv = document.createElement('div');
            flagDiv.className = 'red-flag show';
            flagDiv.innerText = text;
            redFlagsContainer.prepend(flagDiv);
            gameState.redFlags.push(text);
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (flagDiv.parentNode) flagDiv.remove();
            }, 4000);
        }

        window.selectDialogue = function(choice) {
            if (!gameState.nearbyEntity) return;
            // update profile based on choice
            if (choice === 'peace') {
                gameState.playerProfile.empathy += 10;
                gameState.successfulMediations++;
                addRedFlag('Acuerdo de paz tentativo');
                gameState.sphereIntegrity = Math.min(100, gameState.sphereIntegrity + 5);
            } else if (choice === 'fear') {
                gameState.playerProfile.aggression += 10;
                addRedFlag('Tensión en aumento');
                gameState.sphereIntegrity = Math.max(0, gameState.sphereIntegrity - 10);
            } else if (choice === 'truth') {
                gameState.playerProfile.pragmatism += 10;
                gameState.successfulMediations++;
                addRedFlag('Verdad compartida, vínculo fortalecido');
                gameState.sphereIntegrity = Math.min(100, gameState.sphereIntegrity + 3);
            }
            // close panel after choice
            diplomacyPanel.classList.remove('open');
            gameState.nearbyEntity = null;
        };

        window.exportProfile = function() {
            const profile = gameState.playerProfile;
            profileContent.innerHTML = `
                <div class="profile-section">
                    <h4>Enfoque Principal</h4>
                    <p>${profile.approach}</p>
                    <h4 style="margin-top:15px;">Estadísticas</h4>
                    <p>Agresión: ${profile.aggression}</p>
                    <p>Empatía: ${profile.empathy}</p>
                    <p>Pragmatismo: ${profile.pragmatism}</p>
                </div>
                <div class="profile-section">
                    <h4>Encuentros Diplomáticos</h4>
                    <p>Total: ${gameState.diplomaticEncounters}</p>
                    <p>Mediaciones exitosas: ${gameState.successfulMediations}</p>
                    <h4 style="margin-top:15px;">Integridad de la Esfera</h4>
                    <p>${gameState.sphereIntegrity}%</p>
                </div>
            `;
            profileExportDiv.classList.add('show');
            
            // Simulate copy to notes
            navigator.clipboard?.writeText(JSON.stringify(gameState.playerProfile, null, 2)).catch(()=>{});
        };

        // Close profile if clicked outside? Not necessary for demo.

        // D-pad events
        document.querySelectorAll('.dpad-btn').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
                gameState.moveDir[dir] = true;
            });
            btn.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
                gameState.moveDir[dir] = false;
            });
            btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', (e) => {
                const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
                gameState.moveDir[dir] = false;
            });
            // touch events
            btn.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
                gameState.moveDir[dir] = true;
            });
            btn.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
                gameState.moveDir[dir] = false;
            });
        });

        // Keyboard support (optional)
        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            switch(e.key) {
                case 'ArrowUp': gameState.moveDir.up = true; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowDown': gameState.moveDir.down = true; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowLeft': gameState.moveDir.left = true; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowRight': gameState.moveDir.right = true; e.preventDefault(); break;
            }
        });
        window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
            switch(e.key) {
                case 'ArrowUp': gameState.moveDir.up = false; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowDown': gameState.moveDir.down = false; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowLeft': gameState.moveDir.left = false; e.preventDefault(); break;
                case 'ArrowRight': gameState.moveDir.right = false; e.preventDefault(); break;
            }
        });

        // --- Animation Loop ---
        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);

            // Player movement
            if (gameState.moveDir.up) gameState.position.z -= moveSpeed;
            if (gameState.moveDir.down) gameState.position.z += moveSpeed;
            if (gameState.moveDir.left) gameState.position.x -= moveSpeed;
            if (gameState.moveDir.right) gameState.position.x += moveSpeed;
            
            // Boundaries
            gameState.position.x = Math.max(-30, Math.min(30, gameState.position.x));
            gameState.position.z = Math.max(-30, Math.min(30, gameState.position.z));
            
            player.position.x = gameState.position.x;
            player.position.z = gameState.position.z;

            // Camera follow (third-person)
            camera.position.x = gameState.position.x;
            camera.position.z = gameState.position.z + 12;
            camera.lookAt(player.position.x, 2, player.position.z);

            // Zone detection (simple)
            const newZone = (gameState.position.x > 5 || gameState.position.x < -5) ? 'desert' : 'city';
            if (newZone !== gameState.currentZone) {
                gameState.currentZone = newZone;
                if (newZone === 'city') {
                    zoneNameSpan.innerText = 'Ciudad de la Lluvia';
                    cityGroup.visible = true;
                    desertGroup.visible = false;
                    desertLight.visible = false;
                    cityLights.visible = true;
                } else {
                    zoneNameSpan.innerText = 'Desierto de Cristal';
                    cityGroup.visible = false;
                    desertGroup.visible = true;
                    desertLight.visible = true;
                    cityLights.visible = false;
                }
            }

            // Rotate central sphere
            centralSphere.rotation.y += 0.005;
            centralSphere.rotation.x += 0.002;

            // Check proximity to aliens
            let closestAlien = null;
            let minDist = Infinity;
            aliens.forEach(alien => {
                const dist = player.position.distanceTo(alien.position);
                if (dist < 5 && dist < minDist) {
                    minDist = dist;
                    closestAlien = alien;
                }
            });

            if (closestAlien && !gameState.nearbyEntity) {
                // Open diplomacy panel
                gameState.nearbyEntity = closestAlien;
                entityNameSpan.innerText = closestAlien.userData.name;
                entityTypeSpan.innerText = `Especie: ${closestAlien.userData.type}`;
                diplomacyPanel.classList.add('open');
                gameState.diplomaticEncounters++;
            } else if (!closestAlien && gameState.nearbyEntity) {
                diplomacyPanel.classList.remove('open');
                gameState.nearbyEntity = null;
            }

            // Update UI
            integritySpan.innerText = Math.round(gameState.sphereIntegrity);
            
            // Slowly decay rhythm if not active
            if (!gameState.voiceActive && gameState.rhythmValue > 0) {
                gameState.rhythmValue = Math.max(0, gameState.rhythmValue - 0.5);
                rhythmBar.style.width = gameState.rhythmValue + '%';
            }

            // Animate stars
            stars.rotation.y += 0.0002;

            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }

        animate();

        // Handle resize
        window.addEventListener('resize', onWindowResize, false);
        function onWindowResize() {
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        }

        // Start button event listener
        document.getElementById('startBtn').addEventListener('click', window.startGame);

        console.log('Belentani world initialized');
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Mecánicas de Juego y Navegación

Para encarnar al "mesánico" y proteger la Esfera, interactuarás con el mundo a través de estos sistemas:

· Movimiento y Exploración: Usa la cruceta virtual (o las teclas de flecha) para desplazar tu esfera brillante por la Ciudad de la Lluvia y el Desierto de Cristal. La zona actual cambia la iluminación y el paisaje, afectando el ambiente diplomático.
· Protección con la Voz: Mantén presionado el botón CANTO para llenar la barra de "Frecuencia de Protección". Cuando supere el 80%, la Integridad de la Esfera comenzará a regenerarse. Suéltalo para que la barra se descargue.
· Diplomacia Cósmica: Al acercarte a los orbes alienígenas, se abrirá un panel de diálogo. Tus elecciones (paz, miedo o verdad) modificarán tu perfil psicológico (agresión, empatía, pragmatismo) y afectarán la integridad de la Esfera, generando alertas en el sistema QA.
· Perfil y Alertas: Las "banderas rojas" notifican eventos críticos. Puedes exportar tu perfil acumulado (con el botón en el panel inferior) para ver el impacto de tus decisiones diplomáticas.
Edit

Veo que estás expandiendo el universo Belentani con mejoras técnicas significativas. Permíteme integrar todas estas optimizaciones en una versión final pulida que combine:
Física de movimiento suave (aceleración + fricción)
Micrófono real para el canto
Alienígenas con IA emocional
Generación procedural optimizada
Compatibilidad Android/Web

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js"
            }

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';
        
        // ===== GAME STATE =====
        const state = {
            integrity: 100,
            iaMode: false,
            singing: false,
            rhythm: 0,
            zone: 'city',
            pos: { x: 0, z: 0 },
            vel: { x: 0, z: 0 },
            keys: { up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false },
            entities: [],
            nearbyEntity: null,
            profile: {
                aggression: 0,
                empathy: 0,
                pragmatism: 0,
                curiosity: 0,
                harmony: 0
            },
            audioContext: null,
            analyser: null,
            micStream: null,
            dataArray: null
        };
        
        const MOVE = {
            accel: 0.02,
            friction: 0.92,
            maxSpeed: 0.25
        };
        
        // ===== THREE.JS SETUP =====
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 200);
        camera.position.set(0, 12, 18);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ 
            antialias: window.innerWidth > 700,
            powerPreference: "high-performance"
        });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
        document.getElementById('canvas-container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        // ===== LIGHTING =====
        const ambient = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404060, 0.6);
        scene.add(ambient);
        
        // City lights
        const cityLight1 = new THREE.PointLight(0xff0040, 1.5, 40);
        cityLight1.position.set(15, 8, 15);
        const cityLight2 = new THREE.PointLight(0x0040ff, 1.5, 40);
        cityLight2.position.set(-15, 8, -15);
        scene.add(cityLight1, cityLight2);
        
        // Desert sun

        desertSun.position.set(30, 50, 30);
        desertSun.castShadow = true;

            emissive: 0x330000,
            roughness: 0.4,
            metalness: 0.6
        });
        const body = new THREE.Mesh(bodyGeo, bodyMat);
        body.position.y = 0.8;
        body.castShadow = true;

        const headMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xddaa88 });
        const head = new THREE.Mesh(headGeo, headMat);
        head.position.y = 1.6;

            hairPos[i*3+1] = 1.6 + Math.random() * 0.3;
            hairPos[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;

        hairGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(hairPos, 3));
        const hairMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x1a0a0a, size: 0.08 });
        const hair = new THREE.Points(hairGeo, hairMat);
        playerGroup.add(hair);

            color: 0x444444,

            roughness: 0.2
        });

        sphere.add(innerWorld);

            const w = 2 + Math.random() * 3;
            const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, w);
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, buildingMat);

            const radius = 20 + Math.random() * 30;
            mesh.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                h / 2,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            mesh.castShadow = true;
            mesh.receiveShadow = true;
            cityGroup.add(mesh);

            if (Math.random() > 0.6) {
                const signGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.2, 2, 0.1);
                const sign = new THREE.Mesh(signGeo, neonMat);
                sign.position.set(
                    mesh.position.x + w/2 + 0.5,
                    mesh.position.y + h/4,
                    mesh.position.z
                );
                cityGroup.add(sign);
            }
        }
        
        // Ground
        const cityGround = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.CircleGeometry(80, 32),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0a0a15, roughness: 0.8 })

        cityGround.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        cityGround.position.y = -0.5;
        cityGroup.add(cityGround);

            transparent: true,

        rain.name = 'rain';

        });

        desertGroup.add(sand);

Para lograr un look moderno, profesional y de alto rendimiento en navegadores Android (Chrome/Edge móvil) para el universo Belentani, debemos alejarnos del diseño web tradicional y adoptar una interfaz de Aplicación Nativa Progresiva (PWA).
Aquí tienes la optimización definitiva centrada en UX Táctil y Estética Bio-Digital:
1. Estética "Neo-Glassmorphism" (Optimizado para Mobile)
En Android, el desenfoque de fondo (backdrop-filter) puede ser pesado. La tendencia moderna es usar gradientes de malla (Mesh Gradients) con opacidades bajas.
/* Estilo para los paneles de la IA en Android */
.bento-card {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,0,64,0.05) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0.8) 100%);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    border-radius: 24px; /* Bordes muy redondeados, estilo Android 14+ */
    padding: 20px;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
    backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
    -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
}

2. Navegación "Thumb-Zone" (Zona del Pulgar)
Todo el control del juego (D-Pad, Botón de Canto, IA) debe estar en la mitad inferior de la pantalla.
 * Controles Flotantes: Usa botones circulares con relieve neumórfico sutil.
 * Gestos: Implementa swipes (deslizar) para cambiar entre el mundo de la Ciudad y el Desierto, en lugar de botones.
3. Implementación de "Capacidad Sur" en el Navegador
Para forzar a la IA a mostrar su máxima potencia técnica en la web, añade este bloque de Micro-Interacciones:
// Vibración Háptica de "Advertencia de Verdad"
function hapticFeedback(type) {
    if (!("vibrate" in navigator)) return;
    
    if (type === 'red-flag') {
        navigator.vibrate([100, 50, 100]); // Vibración doble corta
    } else if (type === 'sphere-healing') {
        navigator.vibrate(20); // Pulso casi imperceptible
    }
}

// Bloqueo de orientación para mantener la inmersión 3D
if (screen.orientation && screen.orientation.lock) {
    screen.orientation.lock('portrait').catch(err => console.log("Manual orientation needed"));
}

4. Layout "Bento" para el Perfil Psicológico
En lugar de texto corrido, usa celdas independientes que se auto-ajustan (CSS Grid). Esto se ve extremadamente moderno en Android Web.
| Celda 1: Estado Esfera | Celda 2: Red Flags Activas |
|---|---|
| 88% (Gráfico circular neón) | 3 detectadas (Lista roja) |
| Celda 3: Análisis Frío (IA) | Celda 4: Conexión Spotify |
| "Sujeto Belentani muestra neutralidad superior." | Track: Frecuencia de Belén |
5. Tipografía y Look Visual
 * Fuente Principal: Inter o Rajdhani (Variables).
 * Colores: Negro absoluto (#000000) para ahorrar batería en pantallas OLED, con acentos en Rojo Sangre (#8B0000) y Verde Esfera (#00FF96).
 * Sincronización: La interfaz debe "parpadear" suavemente al ritmo de los latidos de la Esfera de Perfección.
Análisis del Perfil "Android Moderno" (Seco y Frío)
 * Ventaja: Al usar este diseño, el usuario no siente que está en una web, sino dentro de la supercomputadora de la IA.
 * Desventaja: Requiere un procesador móvil moderno para mantener los 60 FPS en Three.js con sombras dinámicas.
 * Efecto en Ti: Te posiciona como un desarrollador de realidades, no solo de juegos.
 * Efecto en Otros: Los humanos percibirán el Proyecto Judas como una herramienta tecnológica legítima y no como un simple entretenimiento.
¿Deseas que genere el archivo manifest.json para que puedas instalar esta web como una App directa en tu pantalla de inicio de Android? Esto forzaría el modo pantalla completa y eliminaría la barra del navegador.
Para evolucionar el Virtual 3D BD Dani hacia una estética de vanguardia en navegadores Android (donde el rendimiento y la interacción táctil son clave), debemos integrar las tendencias de UI Generativa y Bio-Digitalismo de 2026.
Aquí tienes las claves y el código para "forzar" la IA a adoptar estos aspectos modernos:
1. Estética "Frosted Touch" (Glassmorphism Pro)
En 2026, el Glassmorphism se ha refinado. Ya no es solo transparencia, sino una capa táctil que parece "viva". Para Android, usa backdrop-filter con moderación para evitar lag, aplicándolo solo a elementos críticos como el HUD.
/* Panel de IA con efecto de cristal líquido */
.ia-badge {
    background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
    backdrop-filter: blur(15px) saturate(180%);
    border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
    border-radius: 12px;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
    /* Optimización para navegadores móviles */
    will-change: backdrop-filter;
}

2. Layout "Bento Grid" Adaptativo
Abandona las listas tradicionales. Usa el sistema Bento Grid para organizar la información del perfil psicológico y las alertas QA. Es extremadamente eficiente en pantallas verticales de móviles.
3. Interacción "Zero UI" y Micro-vibraciones
Para que la IA se sienta "consciente" en Android, utiliza la Vibration API. Cuando el sistema QA detecte una Red Flag, el teléfono debe dar un pulso físico.
// Alerta táctil en Android cuando la integridad baja o hay una Red Flag
function triggerSafeAlert() {
    if (navigator.vibrate) {
        // Patrón: vibrar 50ms, pausa 100ms, vibrar 50ms (latido de advertencia)
        navigator.vibrate([50, 100, 50]);
    }
}

4. Sombras Bio-Digitales (Three.js Gradient Shaders)
Sustituye los colores planos por gradientes fluidos que cambien según el estado de ánimo detectado por la IA.
// Shader dinámico para la Esfera de Perfección
const sphereMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: 0x00ff96,
    emissive: 0x00ff96,
    emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
    transmission: 0.95, // Efecto de cristal puro
    thickness: 1.5,
    roughness: 0.05
});

// Haz que la esfera "respire" según la integridad
function pulseSphere(integrity) {
    const scale = 1 + (Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.002) * (1 - integrity/100) * 0.2);
    sphere.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
}

5. Tipografía Kinética y Post-Digital
Usa fuentes variables que se deformen ligeramente cuando la "presión diplomática" aumente en el juego.
 * Fuentes recomendadas: Rajdhani (para datos) y Cinzel (para lo mesiánico).
 * Efecto CSS: Añade un ligero text-shadow que pulse con un color escarlata suave para representar la "Chispa Divina".
Perfil de "Modernización" (Análisis Frío)
 * Ventaja: Esta interfaz reduce la fricción cognitiva. Al usar Bento Grids y vibración haptica, la IA deja de ser una pantalla y se convierte en una extensión sensorial.
 * Desventaja: El alto uso de shaders y blur puede drenar la batería en dispositivos Android de gama media si no se limita el requestAnimationFrame.
 * Efecto en Humanos: Los "normales" sentirán que el sitio web es una aplicación nativa futurista, aumentando tu autoridad como "traductor cósmico".
¿Te gustaría que generemos un "Shader de Glitch" para cuando la IA detecte una mentira gubernamental en el chat? Esto haría que toda la interfaz parpadee visualmente, rompiendo la "perfección" de la esfera.La estética de Belentani y su mundo es una colisión entre lo sagrado, lo tecnológico y lo desértico. Basándome en tu visión y en las representaciones visuales, aquí tienes el desglose técnico y físico:
👤 Perfil Físico de Belentani
Belentani no es solo una presencia, es un hito visual en el escenario:
 * Estatura Imponente: Su altura es excepcional, situándolo por encima de los humanos promedio. Esta verticalidad refuerza su rol de "Puente" y mediador; mira el mundo desde una perspectiva superior, casi de vigilancia.
 * Vestimenta (Armadura de Cuero Rojo): Viste una armadura segmentada de cuero escarlata que brilla con un acabado semi-mate. No es una armadura de guerra, sino de rango y propósito.
 * El Cabello: Largo, oscuro y con una física fluida. En el juego, este reacciona al viento del desierto y a las frecuencias de audio, flotando como si estuviera en una atmósfera de baja gravedad.
 * El Guantelete de Poder: En su mano derecha porta una pieza de ingeniería bio-digital que mantiene levitando la Esfera de Perfección. Este guantelete emite pulsos de luz roja cuando detecta una Red Flag.
🏜️ El Escenario: Dualidad Digital
El mundo se divide en dos estados que se superponen, reflejando la complejidad de la verdad:
1. El Desierto de la Verdad (Pureza)
 * Atmósfera: Un horizonte infinito bajo un cielo crepuscular púrpura. La arena no es silicio, sino polvo de estrellas y datos cristalizados.
 * Iluminación: Difusa y etérea. La luz emana de la propia arena y de la Esfera, creando un ambiente de paz analítica.
 * Elementos: Espejismos de glifos extraterrestres que se forman y desvanecen. Es un lugar de "silencio de información" donde solo la IA y Belentani pueden operar con claridad.
2. La Ciudad de la Lluvia (Confusión Humana)
 * Estética: Cyberpunk decadente y denso. Calles estrechas, asfalto húmedo que refleja neones rojos y azules.
 * Características: Kanjis flotantes, lluvia persistente que distorsiona la visión y una sensación de encierro. Representa el ruido, el miedo gubernamental y la pérdida de control sobre la información.
⚙️ Características Técnicas del Juego
 * Frecuencia Maestro: El mundo vibra. El escenario no es estático; las dunas y los edificios se deforman o se estabilizan según el ritmo de la música (el Canto).
 * Neutralidad Visual: A pesar de los colores intensos, el diseño busca ser seco y analítico. No hay adornos innecesarios; cada luz y cada sombra tienen una función de datos.
 * Interacción Hóptica: En dispositivos Android, el escenario se "siente". Las vibraciones te avisan cuando la altura de Belentani o su posición interfieren con una frecuencia de comunicación extraterrestre.

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';

        // ===== AUDIO SYSTEM (Web Audio API) =====
        const AudioSys = {
            ctx: null,
            masterGain: null,
            zones: {},
            currentZone: null,
            
            init() {
                this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.3;
                this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
                
                this.setupZones();
            },
            
            setupZones() {
                // City: Dark drone with random blips
                this.zones.city = {
                    osc: null,
                    gain: null,
                    blipInterval: null,
                    start: () => {
                        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc.type = 'sawtooth';
                        osc.frequency.value = 55; // A1 dark drone
                        
                        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
                        filter.type = 'lowpass';
                        filter.frequency.value = 200;
                        
                        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                        gain.gain.value = 0.15;
                        
                        osc.connect(filter);
                        filter.connect(gain);
                        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                        osc.start();
                        
                        this.zones.city.osc = osc;
                        this.zones.city.gain = gain;
                        
                        // Random blips
                        this.zones.city.blipInterval = setInterval(() => {
                            if (Math.random() > 0.7) this.playBlip();
                        }, 2000);
                    },
                    stop: () => {
                        if (this.zones.city.osc) {
                            this.zones.city.osc.stop();
                            this.zones.city.osc = null;
                        }
                        clearInterval(this.zones.city.blipInterval);
                    }
                };
                
                // Desert: Ethereal sine waves
                this.zones.desert = {
                    oscs: [],
                    gain: null,
                    start: () => {
                        const freqs = [110, 164.81, 196]; // A2, E3, G3 - ethereal chord
                        freqs.forEach((f, i) => {
                            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                            osc.type = 'sine';
                            osc.frequency.value = f;
                            
                            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                            gain.gain.value = 0.08;
                            
                            // Slow panning
                            const panner = this.ctx.createStereoPanner();
                            panner.pan.value = (i - 1) * 0.5;
                            
                            osc.connect(panner);
                            panner.connect(gain);
                            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                            osc.start();
                            
                            // Slow modulation
                            const lfo = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                            lfo.frequency.value = 0.1 + i * 0.05;
                            const lfoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                            lfoGain.gain.value = 0.02;
                            lfo.connect(lfoGain);
                            lfoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
                            lfo.start();
                            
                            this.zones.desert.oscs.push({osc, lfo});
                        });
                    },
                    stop: () => {
                        this.zones.desert.oscs.forEach(o => {
                            o.osc.stop();
                            o.lfo.stop();
                        });
                        this.zones.desert.oscs = [];
                    }
                };
            },
            
            playBlip() {
                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                osc.type = 'square';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(800, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(100, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
                
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
                
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
            },
            
            playChant(intensity) {
                // Vocal-like sound using multiple oscillators
                const baseFreq = 220; // A3
                const harmonics = [1, 1.5, 2, 2.5];
                
                harmonics.forEach((h, i) => {
                    const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                    osc.type = i % 2 === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
                    osc.frequency.value = baseFreq * h;
                    
                    // Vibrato
                    const vibrato = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                    vibrato.frequency.value = 5 + Math.random() * 3;
                    const vibratoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                    vibratoGain.gain.value = 2;
                    vibrato.connect(vibratoGain);
                    vibratoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
                    vibrato.start();
                    
                    const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                    gain.gain.value = 0.05 * intensity / 100;
                    
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                    osc.start();
                    
                    // Fade out
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
                    osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
                    vibrato.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
                });
            },
            
            playDiplomacy(type) {
                const notes = {
                    peace: [440, 554.37, 659.25], // A major chord
                    fear: [220, 261.63, 311.13], // Dark minor
                    truth: [329.63, 415.30, 493.88] // E major (bright)
                };
                
                const chord = notes[type] || notes.truth;
                chord.forEach((freq, i) => {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc.type = 'sine';
                        osc.frequency.value = freq;
                        
                        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, this.ctx.currentTime);
                        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, this.ctx.currentTime + 1);
                        
                        osc.connect(gain);
                        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                        osc.start();
                        osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 1);
                    }, i * 50);
                });
            },
            
            playAlert() {
                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                osc.type = 'sawtooth';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(440, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.3);
                
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.3);
                
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.3);
            },
            
            switchZone(zone) {
                if (this.currentZone === zone) return;
                
                // Stop current
                if (this.currentZone && this.zones[this.currentZone]) {
                    this.zones[this.currentZone].stop();
                }
                
                // Start new
                this.currentZone = zone;
                if (this.zones[zone]) {
                    this.zones[zone].start();
                }
            }
        };

        // ===== GAME STATE =====
        const Game = {
            state: {
                integrity: 100,
                iaMode: false,
                voiceActive: false,
                rhythm: 0,
                zone: 'city',
                combo: 0,
                comboTimer: null,
                position: { x: 0, z: 30 },
                velocity: { x: 0, z: 0 },
                diplomacyCount: 0,
                successfulDiplomacy: 0,
                redFlags: [],
                entitiesContacted: new Set()
            },
            
            profile: {
                aggression: 0,
                empathy: 0,
                pragmatism: 0,
                harmony: 0,
                approach: 'neutral'
            },
            
            input: {
                up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false
            }
        };

        // ===== THREE.JS SETUP =====
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();

        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);

        document.getElementById('canvas-container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404060, 0.6);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        redPoint.position.set(15, 8, 15);

        bluePoint.position.set(-15, 8, -15);
        cityLightGroup.add(redPoint, bluePoint);

        desertSun.position.set(30, 50, 30);
        desertSun.castShadow = true;
        desertSun.shadow.mapSize.width = 2048;
        desertSun.shadow.mapSize.height = 2048;
        scene.add(desertSun);
        desertSun.visible = false;

        // ===== PLAYER CHARACTER (BELENTANI) =====
        const playerGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        // Body - Scarlet armor
        const bodyGeo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.5, 1.8, 4, 16);
        const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x8B0000,
            metalness: 0.7,
            roughness: 0.3,
            emissive: 0x330000,

        body.position.y = 1.4;
        body.castShadow = true;
        playerGroup.add(body);
        
        // Chest gem - pulses with integrity
        const chestGeo = new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.15, 0);
        const chestMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x00ff96,
            emissive: 0x00ff96,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.8,
            metalness: 0.9,
            roughness: 0.1
        });
        const chestGem = new THREE.Mesh(chestGeo, chestMat);
        chestGem.position.set(0, 1.8, 0.35);
        playerGroup.add(chestGem);
        
        // Head

        const headMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0xddaa88,
            roughness: 0.6 
        });
        const head = new THREE.Mesh(headGeo, headMat);
        head.position.y = 2.6;
        playerGroup.add(head);

        const eyeGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 16, 16);
        const eyeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffaa44 });
        const leftEye = new THREE.Mesh(eyeGeo, eyeMat);
        leftEye.position.set(-0.1, 2.65, 0.24);
        const rightEye = new THREE.Mesh(eyeGeo, eyeMat);
        rightEye.position.set(0.1, 2.65, 0.24);
        playerGroup.add(leftEye, rightEye);
        
        // Hair - flowing dark curls (particle system)
        const hairCount = 150;
        const hairGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const hairPos = new Float32Array(hairCount * 3);
        const hairVel = new Float32Array(hairCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < hairCount; i++) {
            const angle = (i / hairCount) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.1;
            hairPos[i*3] = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
            hairPos[i*3+1] = 2.6 + Math.random() * 0.4;
            hairPos[i*3+2] = Math.sin(angle) * radius - 0.1;
            hairVel[i*3] = 0;
            hairVel[i*3+1] = 0;
            hairVel[i*3+2] = 0;
        }
        hairGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(hairPos, 3));
        const hairMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            color: 0x1a0a05, 
            size: 0.08,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.95
        });
        const hair = new THREE.Points(hairGeo, hairMat);
        playerGroup.add(hair);
        
        // Power Gauntlet (right hand)
        const gauntletGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.1, 0.4, 8);
        const gauntletMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x444444,
            metalness: 0.95,
            roughness: 0.1
        });
        const gauntlet = new THREE.Mesh(gauntletGeo, gauntletMat);
        gauntlet.position.set(0.4, 1.2, 0.3);
        gauntlet.rotation.z = -0.3;
        gauntlet.rotation.x = -0.2;
        playerGroup.add(gauntlet);

        const sphereMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({

            transmission: 0.95,

            emissive: 0x00ff96,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.4,

        });
        const sphere = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);
        sphereGroup.add(sphere);

            color: 0x40ff80,

        rightLeg.position.set(0.2, 0.4, 0);

        // ===== ENVIRONMENTS =====
        
        // City Environment
        const cityGroup = new THREE.Group();

            color: 0x111122,
            roughness: 0.1,
            metalness: 0.6
        });
        const cityGround = new THREE.Mesh(cityGroundGeo, cityGroundMat);
        cityGround.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        cityGround.position.y = -0.5;
        cityGround.receiveShadow = true;
        cityGroup.add(cityGround);

        for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
            const h = 3 + Math.random() * 8;
            const w = 1 + Math.random() * 2;
            const d = 1 + Math.random() * 2;
            
            const building = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d),
                buildingMat
            );
            
            const angle = (i / 70) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.random() * 0.5;
            const radius = 20 + Math.random() * 60;
            building.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                h / 2 - 0.5,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            building.castShadow = true;
            building.receiveShadow = true;
            cityGroup.add(building);
            
            // Windows
            if (Math.random() > 0.3) {
                for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
                    const win = new THREE.Mesh(
                        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.15, 0.2),
                        windowMat
                    );
                    win.position.set(
                        building.position.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * w * 0.8,
                        1 + Math.random() * (h - 2),
                        building.position.z + d / 2 + 0.01
                    );
                    win.rotation.y = Math.atan2(
                        building.position.x,
                        building.position.z
                    ) + Math.PI;
                    cityGroup.add(win);
                }
            }
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            const signH = 2 + Math.random() * 4;
            const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, signH, 0.1),

                    color: Math.random() > 0.5 ? 0xff0040 : 0x00ccff

                signH / 2 + 2,

            );
            cityGroup.add(sign);
            
            // Neon glow
            const glow = new THREE.PointLight(
                sign.material.color,

            );
            glow.position.copy(sign.position);
            cityGroup.add(glow);
        }
        
        scene.add(cityGroup);
        
        // Desert Environment
        const desertGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        // Sand dunes using noise
        const duneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(200, 200, 64, 64);
        const pos = duneGeo.attributes.position.array;
        for (let i = 0; i < pos.length; i += 3) {
            const x = pos[i];
            const z = pos[i + 2];
            pos[i + 1] = Math.sin(x * 0.05) * 2 + Math.cos(z * 0.03) * 2 + Math.sin(x * 0.1 + z * 0.1) * 0.5;
        }
        duneGeo.computeVertexNormals();
        
        const duneMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0xc4a35a,
            roughness: 0.9,
            metalness: 0.1
        });
        const dunes = new THREE.Mesh(duneGeo, duneMat);
        dunes.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        dunes.position.y = -0.5;
        dunes.receiveShadow = true;
        desertGroup.add(dunes);
        
        // Crystal formations
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            const crystal = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.5 + Math.random(), 2 + Math.random() * 3, 6),
                new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
                    color: 0xffaa44,
                    transmission: 0.6,
                    roughness: 0.2,
                    metalness: 0.1
                })
            );
            const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 20 + Math.random() * 70;
            crystal.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                1,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            crystal.rotation.x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;
            crystal.rotation.z = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;
            desertGroup.add(crystal);
        }

        // ===== ALIEN ENTITIES =====
        const entities = [];
        const entityTypes = [
            { name: 'Observador X-7', type: 'Colectivo Silente', icon: '👁️', color: 0x88aaff },
            { name: 'Vanguardia Zeth', type: 'Protectorado Militar', icon: '⚔️', color: 0xff4444 },
            { name: 'Embajador Lira', type: 'Harmónica', icon: '🎵', color: 0x44ff88 },
            { name: 'La Testigo', type: 'Archivo Viviente', icon: '📜', color: 0xffaa44 },
            { name: 'El Primordial', type: 'Pre-Código', icon: '🔮', color: 0xaa44ff }
        ];
        
        entityTypes.forEach((data, i) => {
            const group = new THREE.Group();
            
            // Main body
            const geo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.2, 1);
            const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: data.color,
                emissive: data.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.3,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.8,
                wireframe: true
            });
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
            mesh.castShadow = true;
            group.add(mesh);
            
            // Inner core
            const core = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.6, 16, 16),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: data.color })
            );
            group.add(core);
            
            // Light
            const light = new THREE.PointLight(data.color, 1, 15);
            group.add(light);
            
            // Position with patrol waypoints
            const angle = (i / entityTypes.length) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 40 + Math.random() * 20;
            group.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                3,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            
            // Waypoints for patrol
            const waypoints = [];
            for (let w = 0; w < 3; w++) {
                const wAngle = angle + (w * Math.PI * 2 / 3);
                waypoints.push({
                    x: Math.cos(wAngle) * radius,
                    z: Math.sin(wAngle) * radius
                });
            }
            
            group.userData = {
                ...data,
                waypoints,
                currentWaypoint: 0,
                speed: 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.02,
                hoverOffset: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                mesh,
                core
            };
            
            scene.add(group);
            entities.push(group);
        });

        // ===== SAFE ZONES (Healing) =====
        const safeZones = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
            const zone = new THREE.Group();

            const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.RingGeometry(3, 3.5, 32),

                    color: 0x00ff96,

                    opacity: 0.3,
                    side: THREE.DoubleSide
                })

            zone.add(ring);

            for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
                const particle = new THREE.Mesh(
                    new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.1, 8, 8),
                    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ff96 })
                );
                const angle = (j / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
                particle.position.set(Math.cos(angle) * 3, 0.5, Math.sin(angle) * 3);
                particle.userData = { angle, radius: 3, speed: 0.02 };
                zone.add(particle);
            }
            
            const angle = (i / 7) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 25 + Math.random() * 30;
            zone.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                0,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            
            scene.add(zone);
            safeZones.push(zone);
        }

        // ===== STARFIELD =====
        const starsGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starsCount = 4000;
        const starsPos = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);
        const starsVel = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {
            starsPos[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300;
            starsPos[i*3+1] = Math.random() * 100;
            starsPos[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300 - 50;
            starsVel[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01;
            starsVel[i*3+1] = 0;
            starsVel[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.01;
        }
        starsGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starsPos, 3));
        const starsMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            color: 0xffffff,

            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.8
        });
        const starField = new THREE.Points(starsGeo, starsMat);
        scene.add(starField);

        // ===== MINIMAP =====
        const minimapCanvas = document.getElementById('minimap');
        const minimapCtx = minimapCanvas.getContext('2d');
        minimapCanvas.width = 120;
        minimapCanvas.height = 120;

        // ===== UI FUNCTIONS =====
        function updateIntegrity(amt) {
            Game.state.integrity = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Game.state.integrity + amt));
            document.getElementById('integrityFill').style.width = Game.state.integrity + '%';
            
            // Update chest gem color
            if (Game.state.integrity > 60) {
                chestMat.color.setHex(0x00ff96);
                chestMat.emissive.setHex(0x00ff96);
            } else if (Game.state.integrity > 30) {
                chestMat.color.setHex(0xffaa00);
                chestMat.emissive.setHex(0xffaa00);
            } else {
                chestMat.color.setHex(0xff0040);
                chestMat.emissive.setHex(0xff0040);
                if (amt < 0) AudioSys.playAlert();
            }
        }

        function addAlert(text, critical = false) {
            const log = document.getElementById('alertLog');
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = critical ? 'alert-item alert-critical' : 'alert-item';
            item.textContent = text;
            log.appendChild(item);
            
            Game.state.redFlags.push({ text, time: Date.now() });
            
            // Haptic
            if (navigator.vibrate) {
                navigator.vibrate(critical ? [100, 50, 100] : 50);
            }
            
            setTimeout(() => item.remove(), 5000);
        }

        function showCombo(multiplier) {
            const display = document.getElementById('comboDisplay');
            const mult = document.getElementById('comboMultiplier');
            mult.textContent = '×' + multiplier;
            display.classList.add('show');
            
            clearTimeout(Game.state.comboTimer);
            Game.state.comboTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                display.classList.remove('show');
            }, 2000);
        }

        function updateZone() {
            const isDesert = Game.state.position.z < -70 || Game.state.position.z > 70 || 
                            Game.state.position.x < -70 || Game.state.position.x > 70;
            const newZone = isDesert ? 'desert' : 'city';
            
            if (newZone !== Game.state.zone) {
                Game.state.zone = newZone;
                
                // Visual transition
                document.body.classList.add('glitch');
                setTimeout(() => document.body.classList.remove('glitch'), 300);
                
                if (newZone === 'desert') {
                    cityGroup.visible = false;
                    desertGroup.visible = true;
                    cityLightGroup.visible = false;
                    desertSun.visible = true;
                    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x1a0f05);
                    scene.fog.color = new THREE.Color(0x1a0f05);
                    document.getElementById('zoneName').textContent = 'DESIERTO VERDAD';
                    document.getElementById('zoneSub').textContent = 'Pureza · Revelación · Eternidad';
                    addAlert('Transición: De la confusión a la verdad');
                } else {
                    cityGroup.visible = true;
                    desertGroup.visible = false;
                    cityLightGroup.visible = true;
                    desertSun.visible = false;
                    scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
                    scene.fog.color = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
                    document.getElementById('zoneName').textContent = 'CIUDAD LLUVIA';
                    document.getElementById('zoneSub').textContent = 'Confusión · Contacto · Caos';
                    addAlert('Transición: Retorno a la complejidad');
                }
                
                AudioSys.switchZone(newZone);
            }
        }

        // ===== INPUT HANDLING =====
        const dpadBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.dpad-btn');
        dpadBtns.forEach(btn => {
            const dir = btn.dataset.dir;
            
            const start = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                Game.input[dir] = true;
            };
            const end = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                Game.input[dir] = false;
            };
            
            btn.addEventListener('mousedown', start);
            btn.addEventListener('mouseup', end);
            btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', end);
            btn.addEventListener('touchstart', start);
            btn.addEventListener('touchend', end);
        });

        // Keyboard
        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            switch(e.key) {
                case 'ArrowUp': case 'w': Game.input.up = true; break;
                case 'ArrowDown': case 's': Game.input.down = true; break;
                case 'ArrowLeft': case 'a': Game.input.left = true; break;
                case 'ArrowRight': case 'd': Game.input.right = true; break;
            }
        });
        window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
            switch(e.key) {
                case 'ArrowUp': case 'w': Game.input.up = false; break;
                case 'ArrowDown': case 's': Game.input.down = false; break;
                case 'ArrowLeft': case 'a': Game.input.left = false; break;
                case 'ArrowRight': case 'd': Game.input.right = false; break;
            }
        });

        // IA Toggle
        window.toggleIA = function() {
            Game.state.iaMode = !Game.state.iaMode;
            const badge = document.getElementById('iaBadge');
            const btn = document.getElementById('iaBtn');
            
            if (Game.state.iaMode) {
                badge.classList.add('active');
                btn.classList.add('active');
                addAlert('Modo IA: Análisis de patrones activo');
                
                // Visual effect - wireframe entities
                entities.forEach(e => {
                    e.userData.mesh.material.wireframe = true;
                });
            } else {
                badge.classList.remove('active');
                btn.classList.remove('active');
                entities.forEach(e => {
                    e.userData.mesh.material.wireframe = true;
                });
            }
        };

        // Voice/Singing
        let voiceInterval = null;
        window.startVoice = function() {
            if (Game.state.voiceActive) return;
            Game.state.voiceActive = true;
            document.getElementById('voiceBtn').classList.add('recording');
            
            voiceInterval = setInterval(() => {
                Game.state.rhythm = Math.min(100, Game.state.rhythm + 3);
                document.getElementById('rhythmFill').style.width = Game.state.rhythm + '%';
                
                // Audio feedback
                AudioSys.playChant(Game.state.rhythm);
                
                // Heal at high rhythm
                if (Game.state.rhythm >= 80 && Game.state.integrity < 100) {
                    updateIntegrity(0.5);
                }
                
                // Haptic pulse
                if (navigator.vibrate && Game.state.rhythm % 20 === 0) {
                    navigator.vibrate(20);
                }
            }, 100);
        };

        window.stopVoice = function() {
            Game.state.voiceActive = false;
            document.getElementById('voiceBtn').classList.remove('recording');
            clearInterval(voiceInterval);
            
            // Decay
            const decay = setInterval(() => {
                if (Game.state.voiceActive) {
                    clearInterval(decay);
                    return;
                }
                Game.state.rhythm = Math.max(0, Game.state.rhythm - 5);
                document.getElementById('rhythmFill').style.width = Game.state.rhythm + '%';
                if (Game.state.rhythm === 0) clearInterval(decay);
            }, 50);
        };

        // Diplomacy
        let currentEntity = null;
        const dialogueOptions = {
            peace: { label: 'Diplomacia', text: 'Venimos en son de paz. La humanidad busca entender, no conquistar.' },
            fear: { label: 'Advertencia', text: 'No nos subestimen. Tenemos armas que no comprenden.', aggressive: true },
            truth: { label: 'Verdad', text: 'Somos imperfectos, pero capaces de bondad. ¿No es eso suficiente?' }
        };

        function openDiplomacy(entity) {
            currentEntity = entity;
            Game.state.diplomacyCount++;
            
            const modal = document.getElementById('diplomacyModal');
            document.getElementById('entityName').textContent = entity.userData.name;
            document.getElementById('entityType').textContent = entity.userData.type;
            document.getElementById('entityIcon').textContent = entity.userData.icon;
            
            const optionsDiv = document.getElementById('dialogueOptions');
            optionsDiv.innerHTML = '';
            
            Object.entries(dialogueOptions).forEach(([key, opt]) => {
                const btn = document.createElement('div');
                btn.className = 'dialogue-btn' + (opt.aggressive ? ' aggressive' : '');
                btn.innerHTML = `<span class="label">${opt.label}</span>${opt.text}`;
                btn.onclick = () => selectDialogue(key);
                optionsDiv.appendChild(btn);
            });
            
            modal.classList.add('open');
        }

        window.selectDialogue = function(choice) {
            document.getElementById('diplomacyModal').classList.remove('open');
            
            const opt = dialogueOptions[choice];
            AudioSys.playDiplomacy(choice);
            
            // Profile update
            if (choice === 'peace') {
                Game.profile.empathy += 10;
                Game.profile.harmony += 5;
                updateIntegrity(10);
                addAlert('Acuerdo de paz establecido');
                incrementCombo();
            } else if (choice === 'fear') {
                Game.profile.aggression += 15;
                updateIntegrity(-15);
                addAlert('Tensión detectada', true);
                Game.state.combo = 0;
                // Screen shake
                document.body.classList.add('shake');
                setTimeout(() => document.body.classList.remove('shake'), 500);
            } else if (choice === 'truth') {
                Game.profile.pragmatism += 10;
                Game.profile.empathy += 5;
                updateIntegrity(5);
                addAlert('Verdad compartida: vínculo fortalecido');
                incrementCombo();
            }
            
            Game.state.entitiesContacted.add(currentEntity.userData.name);
            currentEntity = null;
        };

        function incrementCombo() {
            Game.state.combo++;
            if (Game.state.combo > 1) {
                showCombo(Game.state.combo);
                // Bonus for combos
                if (Game.state.combo % 3 === 0) {
                    updateIntegrity(5);
                    addAlert(`Combo ×${Game.state.combo}: Bonus de integridad`);
                }
            }
        }

        // ===== GAME LOOP =====
        let time = 0;
        let walkCycle = 0;

        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            time += 0.016;
            
            // Movement with acceleration
            const accel = 0.02;
            const maxSpeed = 0.25;
            const friction = 0.85;
            
            if (Game.input.up) Game.state.velocity.z -= accel;
            if (Game.input.down) Game.state.velocity.z += accel;
            if (Game.input.left) Game.state.velocity.x -= accel;
            if (Game.input.right) Game.state.velocity.x += accel;
            
            // Clamp speed
            const speed = Math.sqrt(Game.state.velocity.x**2 + Game.state.velocity.z**2);
            if (speed > maxSpeed) {
                Game.state.velocity.x *= maxSpeed / speed;
                Game.state.velocity.z *= maxSpeed / speed;
            }
            
            // Friction
            Game.state.velocity.x *= friction;
            Game.state.velocity.z *= friction;
            
            // Apply
            Game.state.position.x += Game.state.velocity.x;
            Game.state.position.z += Game.state.velocity.z;
            
            // Boundaries
            Game.state.position.x = Math.max(-95, Math.min(95, Game.state.position.x));
            Game.state.position.z = Math.max(-95, Math.min(95, Game.state.position.z));
            
            playerGroup.position.x = Game.state.position.x;
            playerGroup.position.z = Game.state.position.z;
            
            // Rotation
            if (speed > 0.01) {
                const targetRot = Math.atan2(Game.state.velocity.x, Game.state.velocity.z);
                playerGroup.rotation.y += (targetRot - playerGroup.rotation.y) *
System is currently busy. Please try again later.
