System is currently busy. Please try again later.
// Continuación de la función animate()
        playerGroup.rotation.y += (targetRot - playerGroup.rotation.y) * 0.1;
    }

    // Animación de la Esfera y el jugador
    sphereGroup.position.y = 1.7 + Math.sin(time * 2) * 0.1;
    innerWorld.rotation.y += 0.02;
    innerWorld.rotation.z += 0.01;
    
    // Ciclo de caminata (si se mueve)
    if (speed > 0.05) {
        walkCycle += speed * 1.5;
        leftLeg.rotation.x = Math.sin(walkCycle) * 0.5;
        rightLeg.rotation.x = Math.sin(walkCycle + Math.PI) * 0.5;
    } else {
        leftLeg.rotation.x *= 0.8;
        rightLeg.rotation.x *= 0.8;
    }

    // Actualizar Entidades (IA y Patrullaje)
    entities.forEach(entity => {
        const data = entity.userData;
        const target = data.waypoints[data.currentWaypoint];
        const dx = target.x - entity.position.x;
        const dz = target.z - entity.position.z;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz);

        if (dist < 1) {
            data.currentWaypoint = (data.currentWaypoint + 1) % data.waypoints.length;
        } else {
            entity.position.x += (dx / dist) * data.speed;
            entity.position.z += (dz / dist) * data.speed;
        }

        entity.position.y = 3 + Math.sin(time + data.hoverOffset) * 0.5;
        data.mesh.rotation.y += 0.01;
        
        // Detección de proximidad con Belentani
        const distToPlayer = entity.position.distanceTo(playerGroup.position);
        if (distToPlayer < 5 && !document.getElementById('diplomacyModal').classList.contains('open')) {
            openDiplomacy(entity);
        }
    });

    // Actualizar Zonas Seguras
    safeZones.forEach(zone => {
        zone.children.forEach(p => {
            if (p.userData.angle !== undefined) {
                p.userData.angle += p.userData.speed;
                p.position.x = Math.cos(p.userData.angle) * p.userData.radius;
                p.position.z = Math.sin(p.userData.angle) * p.userData.radius;
            }
        });
        
        if (zone.position.distanceTo(playerGroup.position) < 4) {
            updateIntegrity(0.1);
        }
    });

    // Seguimiento de cámara
    const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3(
        playerGroup.position.x,
        playerGroup.position.y + 2,
        playerGroup.position.z
    );
    camera.position.x += (playerGroup.position.x - camera.position.x) * 0.05;
    camera.position.z += (playerGroup.position.z + 25 - camera.position.z) * 0.05;
    camera.lookAt(cameraTarget);

    // Actualizar Minimapa
    updateMinimap();
    updateZone();
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

function updateMinimap() {
    minimapCtx.fillStyle = 'black';
    minimapCtx.fillRect(0, 0, 120, 120);
    
    // Dibujar jugador
    const px = (Game.state.position.x + 100) * (120/200);
    const pz = (Game.state.position.z + 100) * (120/200);
    minimapCtx.fillStyle = '#FF1A1A';
    minimapCtx.beginPath();
    minimapCtx.arc(px, pz, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    minimapCtx.fill();
    
    // Dibujar entidades
    entities.forEach(e => {
        const ex = (e.position.x + 100) * (120/200);
        const ez = (e.position.z + 100) * (120/200);
        minimapCtx.fillStyle = '#00CCFF';
        minimapCtx.fillRect(ex-1, ez-1, 2, 2);
    });
}

// Inicialización final
document.getElementById('startBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    AudioSys.init();
    AudioSys.switchZone('city');
    document.getElementById('startScreen').classList.add('hidden');
    animate();
});

window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
});

    }

    // Animación de cabello (física simple)
    const hairPositions = hair.geometry.attributes.position.array;
    for (let i = 0; i < hairCount; i++) {
        const idx = i * 3;
        const origX = hairPositions[idx];
        const origY = 2.6 + (i % 5) * 0.1;
        const origZ = hairPositions[idx + 2];
        
        // Viento + movimiento del jugador
        const wind = Math.sin(time * 2 + i * 0.1) * 0.05;
        const movement = Game.state.velocity.x * 0.1;
        
        hairPositions[idx] = origX + wind + movement;
        hairPositions[idx + 1] = origY + Math.sin(time * 3 + i) * 0.02;
    }
    hair.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;

    // Actualizar Entidades (IA y Patrullaje)
    entities.forEach(entity => {
        const data = entity.userData;
        const target = data.waypoints[data.currentWaypoint];
        const dx = target.x - entity.position.x;
        const dz = target.z - entity.position.z;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz);

        if (dist < 1) {
            data.currentWaypoint = (data.currentWaypoint + 1) % data.waypoints.length;
        } else {
            entity.position.x += (dx / dist) * data.speed;
            entity.position.z += (dz / dist) * data.speed;
        }

        entity.position.y = 3 + Math.sin(time + data.hoverOffset) * 0.5;
        data.mesh.rotation.y += 0.01;
        data.core.rotation.y -= 0.02;
        
        // Efecto de IA mode
        if (Game.state.iaMode) {
            const hostility = Math.sin(time + entity.id) * 50 + 50;
            data.mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = 0.3 + hostility / 200;
        }
        
        // Detección de proximidad con Belentani
        const distToPlayer = entity.position.distanceTo(playerGroup.position);
        if (distToPlayer < 5 && !currentEntity && !document.getElementById('diplomacyModal').classList.contains('open')) {
            openDiplomacy(entity);
        }
    });

    // Actualizar Zonas Seguras
    safeZones.forEach((zone, idx) => {
        zone.rotation.y += 0.005;
        
        zone.children.forEach((p, i) => {
            if (p.userData.angle !== undefined) {
                p.userData.angle += p.userData.speed;
                p.position.x = Math.cos(p.userData.angle) * p.userData.radius;
                p.position.z = Math.sin(p.userData.angle) * p.userData.radius;
                p.position.y = 0.5 + Math.sin(time * 2 + i) * 0.2;
            }
        });
        
        // Curación
        if (zone.position.distanceTo(playerGroup.position) < 4) {
            updateIntegrity(0.2);
            if (time % 1 < 0.05) {
                addAlert('Zona segura: Recuperando integridad');
            }
        }
    });

    // Starfield animation
    const starPositions = starField.geometry.attributes.position.array;
    for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {
        const idx = i * 3;
        starPositions[idx] += starsVel[idx];
        starPositions[idx + 2] += starsVel[idx + 2];
        
        // Reset if too far
        if (Math.abs(starPositions[idx]) > 150) starPositions[idx] *= -0.9;
        if (Math.abs(starPositions[idx + 2]) > 150) starPositions[idx + 2] *= -0.9;
    }
    starField.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;

    // Seguimiento de cámara suave
    const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3(
        playerGroup.position.x,
        playerGroup.position.y + 2,
        playerGroup.position.z
    );
    camera.position.x += (playerGroup.position.x - camera.position.x) * 0.08;
    camera.position.z += (playerGroup.position.z + 20 - camera.position.z) * 0.08;
    camera.position.y += (12 - camera.position.y) * 0.05;
    camera.lookAt(cameraTarget);

    // Actualizar Minimapa
    updateMinimap();
    
    // Check zone
    updateZone();
    
    // Decay integrity slowly
    if (time % 5 < 0.02 && Game.state.integrity > 0) {
        updateIntegrity(-0.1);
    }
    
    // Game over check
    if (Game.state.integrity <= 0 && !Game.state.gameOver) {
        Game.state.gameOver = true;
        addAlert('CRÍTICO: La Esfera se ha fragmentado', true);
        setTimeout(() => {
            alert('SIMULACIÓN TERMINADA\n\nPerfil Final:\n' + 
                  `Empatía: ${Game.profile.empathy}\n` +
                  `Agresión: ${Game.profile.aggression}\n` +
                  `Pragmatismo: ${Game.profile.pragmatism}\n` +
                  `Entidades Contactadas: ${Game.state.entitiesContacted.size}`);
            location.reload();
        }, 2000);
    }

    renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

function updateMinimap() {
    minimapCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)';
    minimapCtx.fillRect(0, 0, 120, 120);
    
    // Dibujar zonas
    safeZones.forEach(zone => {
        const zx = (zone.position.x + 100) * (120/200);
        const zz = (zone.position.z + 100) * (120/200);
        minimapCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,150,0.3)';
        minimapCtx.beginPath();
        minimapCtx.arc(zx, zz, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        minimapCtx.fill();
    });
    
    // Dibujar entidades
    entities.forEach(e => {
        const ex = (e.position.x + 100) * (120/200);
        const ez = (e.position.z + 100) * (120/200);
        minimapCtx.fillStyle = e.userData.color;
        minimapCtx.fillRect(ex-2, ez-2, 4, 4);
    });
    
    // Dibujar jugador (triángulo direccional)
    const px = (Game.state.position.x + 100) * (120/200);
    const pz = (Game.state.position.z + 100) * (120/200);
    const rot = playerGroup.rotation.y;
    
    minimapCtx.fillStyle = '#FF1A1A';
    minimapCtx.save();
    minimapCtx.translate(px, pz);
    minimapCtx.rotate(-rot);
    minimapCtx.beginPath();
    minimapCtx.moveTo(0, -5);
    minimapCtx.lineTo(-4, 4);
    minimapCtx.lineTo(4, 4);
    minimapCtx.closePath();
    minimapCtx.fill();
    minimapCtx.restore();
    
    // Borde
    minimapCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139,0,0,0.5)';
    minimapCtx.lineWidth = 2;
    minimapCtx.strokeRect(0, 0, 120, 120);
}

Entendido. Aquí está el código COMPLETO Y FINAL de BELENTANI: Guardián de Esferas - sin referencias a BD Dani, puramente centrado en el universo Belentani como entidad mesiánica, guardiana y diplomática cósmica.
HTML
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover">
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#000000">
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes">
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="black-translucent">
    <title>BELENTANI — Guardián de Esferas</title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <style>
        @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cinzel:wght@400;600;700&family=Rajdhani:wght@300;500;600;700&family=Syncopate:wght@400;700&display=swap');
        
        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; }
        
        :root {
            --scarlet: #8B0000;
            --scarlet-bright: #FF1A1A;

        <div class="diplomacy-card">

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';

        // ==========================================
        // AUDIO SYSTEM (Web Audio API)
        // ==========================================
        const AudioSys = {
            ctx: null,
            masterGain: null,
            zones: {},
            currentZone: null,
            reverb: null,
            
            init() {
                this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                this.masterGain.gain.value = 0.25;
                
                // Create reverb for ethereal sound
                this.reverb = this.createReverb();
                
                this.masterGain.connect(this.reverb);
                this.reverb.connect(this.ctx.destination);
                
                this.setupZones();
            },
            
            createReverb() {
                const convolver = this.ctx.createConvolver();
                const rate = this.ctx.sampleRate;
                const length = rate * 2; // 2 seconds
                const impulse = this.ctx.createBuffer(2, length, rate);
                
                for (let channel = 0; channel < 2; channel++) {
                    const data = impulse.getChannelData(channel);
                    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
                        data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / length, 2);
                    }
                }
                
                convolver.buffer = impulse;
                return convolver;
            },
            
            setupZones() {
                // City: Dark industrial drone with random blips
                this.zones.city = {
                    oscs: [],
                    gain: null,
                    blipInterval: null,
                    start: () => {
                        // Main drone
                        const osc1 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc1.type = 'sawtooth';
                        osc1.frequency.value = 55;
                        
                        const osc2 = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc2.type = 'square';
                        osc2.frequency.value = 110;
                        
                        const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
                        filter.type = 'lowpass';
                        filter.frequency.value = 180;
                        filter.Q.value = 5;
                        
                        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                        gain.gain.value = 0.08;
                        
                        osc1.connect(filter);
                        osc2.connect(filter);
                        filter.connect(gain);
                        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                        
                        osc1.start();
                        osc2.start();
                        
                        this.zones.city.oscs = [osc1, osc2];
                        this.zones.city.gain = gain;
                        
                        // Random blips
                        this.zones.city.blipInterval = setInterval(() => {
                            if (Math.random() > 0.6) this.playBlip();
                        }, 1500);
                    },
                    stop: () => {
                        this.zones.city.oscs.forEach(o => o.stop());
                        clearInterval(this.zones.city.blipInterval);
                    }
                };
                
                // Desert: Ethereal sacred tones
                this.zones.desert = {
                    oscs: [],
                    start: () => {
                        const freqs = [110, 164.81, 196, 220]; // Sacred chord
                        freqs.forEach((f, i) => {
                            const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                            osc.type = 'sine';
                            osc.frequency.value = f;
                            
                            const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                            gain.gain.value = 0.04;
                            
                            const panner = this.ctx.createStereoPanner();
                            panner.pan.value = Math.sin(i * Math.PI / 2) * 0.7;
                            
                            // Slow vibrato
                            const vibrato = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                            vibrato.frequency.value = 0.08 + i * 0.03;
                            const vibratoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                            vibratoGain.gain.value = 3;
                            vibrato.connect(vibratoGain);
                            vibratoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
                            vibrato.start();
                            
                            osc.connect(panner);
                            panner.connect(gain);
                            gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                            osc.start();
                            
                            this.zones.desert.oscs.push({osc, vibrato});
                        });
                    },
                    stop: () => {
                        this.zones.desert.oscs.forEach(o => {
                            o.osc.stop();
                            o.vibrato.stop();
                        });
                    }
                };
            },
            
            playBlip() {
                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                osc.type = 'square';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(110, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
                
                const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
                filter.type = 'highpass';
                filter.frequency.value = 400;
                
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
                
                osc.connect(filter);
                filter.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
            },
            
            playChant(intensity) {
                // Multi-voice sacred chant
                const baseFreq = 220;
                const harmonics = [1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3];
                
                harmonics.forEach((h, i) => {
                    const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                    osc.type = i % 2 === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
                    osc.frequency.value = baseFreq * h;
                    
                    // Sacred vibrato
                    const vibrato = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                    vibrato.frequency.value = 4 + Math.random() * 2;
                    const vibratoGain = this.ctx.createGain();
                    vibratoGain.gain.value = 2.5;
                    vibrato.connect(vibratoGain);
                    vibratoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
                    vibrato.start();
                    
                    const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                    gain.gain.value = 0.025 * (intensity / 100) * (1 - i * 0.15);
                    
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                    osc.start();
                    
                    // Fade with reverb tail
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.8);
                    osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.8);
                    vibrato.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.8);
                });
            },
            
            playDiplomacy(type) {
                const chords = {
                    peace: [440, 554.37, 659.25, 880], // A major - harmonious
                    fear: [220, 277.18, 329.63, 415.30], // Dark minor
                    truth: [329.63, 415.30, 493.88, 659.25] // E major - revelation
                };
                
                const chord = chords[type];
                const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
                
                chord.forEach((freq, i) => {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc.type = 'sine';
                        osc.frequency.value = freq;
                        
                        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime);
                        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.05);
                        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 1.2);
                        
                        osc.connect(gain);
                        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                        osc.start();
                        osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 1.2);
                    }, i * 60);
                });
            },
            
            playAlert() {
                const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                osc.type = 'sawtooth';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, this.ctx.currentTime);
                osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(440, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
                
                const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, this.ctx.currentTime);
                gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
                
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                osc.start();
                osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
            },
            
            playResonance() {
                // Special sound for successful alien resonance
                const freqs = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.50]; // C major arpeggio up
                freqs.forEach((f, i) => {
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
                        osc.type = 'sine';
                        osc.frequency.value = f;
                        
                        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
                        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, this.ctx.currentTime);
                        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.6);
                        
                        osc.connect(gain);
                        gain.connect(this.masterGain);
                        osc.start();
                        osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.6);
                    }, i * 80);
                });
            },
            
            switchZone(zone) {
                if (this.currentZone === zone) return;
                if (this.currentZone && this.zones[this.currentZone]) {
                    this.zones[this.currentZone].stop();
                }
                this.currentZone = zone;
                if (this.zones[zone]) this.zones[zone].start();
            }
        };

        // ==========================================
        // GAME STATE
        // ==========================================
        const Game = {
            state: {
                integrity: 100,
                iaMode: false,
                voiceActive: false,
                rhythm: 0,
                zone: 'city',
                combo: 0,
                comboTimer: null,
                position: { x: 0, z: 30 },
                velocity: { x: 0, z: 0 },
                diplomacyCount: 0,
                successfulDiplomacy: 0,
                redFlags: [],
                entitiesContacted: new Set(),
                gameOver: false,
                divineMode: false
            },
            
            profile: {
                aggression: 0,
                empathy: 0,
                pragmatism: 0,
                harmony: 0,
                divineAuthority: 0,
                approach: 'neutral'
            },
            
            input: { up: false, down: false, left: false, right: false }
        };

        // ==========================================
        // THREE.JS SETUP
        // ==========================================
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.012);

        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 300);
        camera.position.set(0, 12, 22);

        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ 
            antialias: true, 
            powerPreference: "high-performance",
            alpha: false
        });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
        renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
        renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.1;
        document.getElementById('canvas-container').appendChild(renderer.domElement);

        // ==========================================
        // LIGHTING
        // ==========================================
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404060, 0.5);
        scene.add(ambientLight);

        // City lights
        const cityLightGroup = new THREE.Group();
        const redPoint = new THREE.PointLight(0xff0040, 2.5, 50);
        redPoint.position.set(20, 10, 20);
        const bluePoint = new THREE.PointLight(0x0040ff, 2.5, 50);
        bluePoint.position.set(-20, 10, -20);
        const purplePoint = new THREE.PointLight(0x8000ff, 1.5, 40);
        purplePoint.position.set(0, 15, -30);
        cityLightGroup.add(redPoint, bluePoint, purplePoint);
        scene.add(cityLightGroup);

        // Desert sun (divine light)
        const desertSun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffaa44, 1.8);
        desertSun.position.set(50, 80, 50);
        desertSun.castShadow = true;
        desertSun.shadow.mapSize.width = 2048;
        desertSun.shadow.mapSize.height = 2048;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.near = 0.5;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.far = 200;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.left = -100;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.right = 100;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.top = 100;
        desertSun.shadow.camera.bottom = -100;
        scene.add(desertSun);
        desertSun.visible = false;

        // Fill light
        const fillLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x6688aa, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-20, 10, 20);
        scene.add(fillLight);

        // ==========================================
        // BELENTANI CHARACTER
        // ==========================================
        const playerGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        // Body - Scarlet divine armor
        const bodyGeo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.55, 2, 4, 16);
        const bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x8B0000,
            metalness: 0.75,
            roughness: 0.25,
            emissive: 0x330000,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.15
        });
        const body = new THREE.Mesh(bodyGeo, bodyMat);
        body.position.y = 1.6;
        body.castShadow = true;
        playerGroup.add(body);
        
        // Chest gem - Divine core (pulses with integrity)
        const chestGeo = new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.18, 0);
        const chestMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x00ff96,
            emissive: 0x00ff96,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.9,
            metalness: 0.95,
            roughness: 0.05
        });
        const chestGem = new THREE.Mesh(chestGeo, chestMat);
        chestGem.position.set(0, 2.1, 0.4);
        playerGroup.add(chestGem);
        
        // Head
        const headGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.32, 32, 32);
        const headMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0xddaa88,
            roughness: 0.6 
        });
        const head = new THREE.Mesh(headGeo, headMat);
        head.position.y = 2.9;
        playerGroup.add(head);
        
        // Eyes - Divine sight (glowing)
        const eyeGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.045, 16, 16);
        const eyeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffaa44 });
        const leftEye = new THREE.Mesh(eyeGeo, eyeMat);
        leftEye.position.set(-0.12, 2.95, 0.28);
        const rightEye = new THREE.Mesh(eyeGeo, eyeMat);
        rightEye.position.set(0.12, 2.95, 0.28);
        playerGroup.add(leftEye, rightEye);
        
        // Hair - Long dark curly (particle system with physics)
        const hairCount = 200;
        const hairGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const hairPos = new Float32Array(hairCount * 3);
        const hairBasePos = [];
        
        for (let i = 0; i < hairCount; i++) {
            const angle = (i / hairCount) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 0.28 + Math.random() * 0.12;
            const y = 2.9 + Math.random() * 0.5 - 0.2;
            const x = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
            const z = Math.sin(angle) * radius - 0.15;
            
            hairPos[i*3] = x;
            hairPos[i*3+1] = y;
            hairPos[i*3+2] = z;
            
            hairBasePos.push({ x, y, z, angle, radius, phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2 });
        }
        
        hairGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(hairPos, 3));
        const hairMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            color: 0x1a0a05, 
            size: 0.09,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.95
        });
        const hair = new THREE.Points(hairGeo, hairMat);
        playerGroup.add(hair);
        
        // Power Gauntlet - Right hand (divine instrument)
        const gauntletGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        const gauntletBase = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.12, 0.5, 8),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: 0x444444,
                metalness: 0.98,
                roughness: 0.08
            })
        );
        gauntletGroup.add(gauntletBase);
        
        // Glowing runes on gauntlet
        const runeGeo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.13, 0.02, 8, 16);
        const runeMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff0040 });
        const rune1 = new THREE.Mesh(runeGeo, runeMat);
        rune1.position.y = 0.15;
        rune1.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
        const rune2 = new THREE.Mesh(runeGeo, runeMat);
        rune2.position.y = -0.15;
        rune2.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
        gauntletGroup.add(rune1, rune2);
        
        gauntletGroup.position.set(0.45, 1.4, 0.35);
        gauntletGroup.rotation.z = -0.4;
        gauntletGroup.rotation.x = -0.3;
        playerGroup.add(gauntletGroup);
        
        // The Sphere of Perfection (levitating above gauntlet)
        const sphereGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.4, 64, 64);
        const sphereMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
            color: 0x00ff96,
            metalness: 0.05,
            roughness: 0,
            transmission: 0.98,
            thickness: 2,
            emissive: 0x00ff96,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
            clearcoat: 1,
            clearcoatRoughness: 0,
            ior: 1.5
        });
        const sphere = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, sphereMat);
        sphereGroup.add(sphere);
        
        // Inner sacred world
        const innerGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.18, 2);
        const innerMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ 
            color: 0x40ff80,
            wireframe: true,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.8
        });
        const innerWorld = new THREE.Mesh(innerGeo, innerMat);
        sphereGroup.add(innerWorld);
        
        // Inner core glow
        const coreGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.08, 16, 16);
        const coreMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ 
            color: 0xffffff,
            emissive: 0xffffff,
            emissiveIntensity: 1
        });
        const innerCore = new THREE.Mesh(coreGeo, coreMat);
        sphereGroup.add(innerCore);
        
        // Sphere light
        const sphereLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ff96, 1.5, 12);
        sphereGroup.add(sphereLight);
        
        // Position relative to gauntlet
        sphereGroup.position.set(0.45, 2.2, 0.6);
        playerGroup.add(sphereGroup);
        
        // Legs with animation
        const legGeo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.16, 0.9, 4, 12);
        const leftLeg = new THREE.Mesh(legGeo, bodyMat);
        leftLeg.position.set(-0.22, 0.45, 0);
        leftLeg.castShadow = true;
        const rightLeg = new THREE.Mesh(legGeo, bodyMat);
        rightLeg.position.set(0.22, 0.45, 0);
        rightLeg.castShadow = true;
        playerGroup.add(leftLeg, rightLeg);
        
        // Divine aura (subtle glow around Belentani)
        const auraGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.5, 32, 32);
        const auraMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
            color: 0xff0040,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.03,
            side: THREE.BackSide
        });
        const aura = new THREE.Mesh(auraGeo, auraMat);
        aura.position.y = 1.5;
        playerGroup.add(aura);
        
        scene.add(playerGroup);

        // ==========================================
        // ENVIRONMENTS
        // ==========================================
        
        // City - Cyberpunk rain
        const cityGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        // Wet asphalt ground
        const cityGroundGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(300, 300);
        const cityGroundMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x0a0a15,
            roughness: 0.05,
            metalness: 0.8
        });
        const cityGround = new THREE.Mesh(cityGroundGeo, cityGroundMat);
        cityGround.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        cityGround.position.y = -0.5;
        cityGround.receiveShadow = true;
        cityGroup.add(cityGround);
        
        // Rain particles
        const rainCount = window.innerWidth < 700 ? 400 : 1000;
        const rainGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const rainPos = new Float32Array(rainCount * 3);
        const rainVel = [];
        
        for (let i = 0; i < rainCount; i++) {
            rainPos[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
            rainPos[i*3+1] = Math.random() * 60;
            rainPos[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
            rainVel.push(0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4);
        }
        
        rainGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(rainPos, 3));
        const rainMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            color: 0x88aaff,
            size: 0.08,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.4
        });
        const rain = new THREE.Points(rainGeo, rainMat);
        cityGroup.add(rain);
        
        // Buildings with neon
        const buildingMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
            color: 0x151525,
            roughness: 0.7,
            metalness: 0.3
        });
        
        const windowColors = [0xffaa44, 0x44aaff, 0xff44aa, 0xaaff44];
        
        for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) {
            const h = 4 + Math.random() * 12;
            const w = 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.5;
            const d = 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.5;
            
            const building = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d),
                buildingMat
            );
            
            const angle = (i / 80) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.random() * 0.8;
            const radius = 25 + Math.random() * 80;
            building.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                h / 2 - 0.5,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            building.castShadow = true;
            building.receiveShadow = true;
            cityGroup.add(building);
            
            // Windows with lights
            const windowCount = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4) + 2;
            for (let j = 0; j < windowCount; j++) {
                if (Math.random() > 0.4) {
                    const winColor = windowColors[Math.floor(Math.random() * windowColors.length)];
                    const win = new THREE.Mesh(
                        new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.2, 0.25),
                        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: winColor })
                    );
                    win.position.set(
                        building.position.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * w * 0.7,
                        1 + Math.random() * (h - 2),
                        building.position.z + d / 2 + 0.02
                    );
                    win.rotation.y = Math.atan2(
                        building.position.x,
                        building.position.z
                    ) + Math.PI;
                    cityGroup.add(win);
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Neon signs
        for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
            const signH = 3 + Math.random() * 6;
            const isRed = Math.random() > 0.4;
            const signColor = isRed ? 0xff0040 : 0x00ccff;
            
            const sign = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.4, signH, 0.15),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: signColor })
            );
            
            const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 30 + Math.random() * 60;
            sign.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                signH / 2 + 3,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            sign.rotation.y = -angle;
            cityGroup.add(sign);
            
            // Neon glow
            const glow = new THREE.PointLight(signColor, 1.5, 15);
            glow.position.copy(sign.position);
            cityGroup.add(glow);
        }
        
        scene.add(cityGroup);
        
        // Desert - Sacred wasteland
        const desertGroup = new THREE.Group();
        
        // Sacred dunes
        const duneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(300, 300, 80, 80);
        const pos = duneGeo.attributes.position.array;
        
        for (let i = 0; i < pos.length; i += 3) {
            const x = pos[i];
            const z = pos[i + 2];
            const noise1 = Math.sin(x * 0.03) * 3;
            const noise2 = Math.cos(z * 0.02) * 2.5;
            const noise3 = Math.sin((x + z) * 0.05) * 1;
            pos[i + 1] = noise1 + noise2 + noise3;
        }
        
        duneGeo.computeVertexNormals();
        
        const duneMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0xd4a574,
            roughness: 0.95,
            metalness: 0.05,
            emissive: 0x332211,
            emissiveIntensity: 0.1
        });
        
        const dunes = new THREE.Mesh(duneGeo, duneMat);
        dunes.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        dunes.position.y = -0.5;
        dunes.receiveShadow = true;
        desertGroup.add(dunes);
        
        // Crystal formations (sacred geometry)
        for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            const crystalType = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
            let crystal;
            
            if (crystalType === 0) {
                crystal = new THREE.Mesh(
                    new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.6 + Math.random(), 3 + Math.random() * 4, 6),
                    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
                        color: 0xffcc88,
                        transmission: 0.7,
                        roughness: 0.1,
                        metalness: 0.1,
                        emissive: 0xffaa44,
                        emissiveIntensity: 0.2
                    })
                );
            } else if (crystalType === 1) {
                crystal = new THREE.Mesh(
                    new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1 + Math.random(), 0),
                    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
                        color: 0x88ccff,
                        transmission: 0.8,
                        roughness: 0.05,
                        emissive: 0x4488ff,
                        emissiveIntensity: 0.15
                    })
                );
            } else {
                crystal = new THREE.Mesh(
                    new THREE.TetrahedronGeometry(1.2 + Math.random(), 0),
                    new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
                        color: 0xff88cc,
                        transmission: 0.6,
                        roughness: 0.15,
                        emissive: 0xff44aa,
                        emissiveIntensity: 0.1
                    })
                );
            }
            
            const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 30 + Math.random() * 100;
            crystal.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                1.5,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            crystal.rotation.x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
            crystal.rotation.z = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
            crystal.castShadow = true;
            desertGroup.add(crystal);
        }
        
        // Sacred obelisks
        for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            const obelisk = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.3, 0.5, 8, 4),
                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                    color: 0x222233,
                    metalness: 0.9,
                    roughness: 0.2,
                    emissive: 0xff0040,
                    emissiveIntensity: 0.1
                })
            );
            
            const angle = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
            const radius = 60;
            obelisk.position.set(
                Math.cos(angle) * radius,
                4,
                Math.sin(angle) * radius
            );
            obelisk.rotation.y = angle + Math.PI / 4;
            desertGroup.add(obelisk);
            
            // Obelisk glow
            const obeliskLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xff0040, 0.8, 20);
            obeliskLight.position.copy(obelisk.position);
            obeliskLight.position.y += 3;
            desertGroup.add(obeliskLight);
        }
        
        desertGroup.visible = false;
        scene.add(desertGroup);

            const group = new THREE.Group();
            
            // Outer shell (wireframe)
            const shellGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.3, 1);
            const shellMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: data.color,
                emissive: data.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.25,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.6,
                wireframe: true
            });
            const shell = new THREE.Mesh(shellGeo, shellMat);

                emissive: data.color,

            const core = new THREE.Mesh(coreGeo, coreMat);

            const radius = 45 + Math.random() * 25;

            };

                const wAngle = angle + (w * Math.PI * 2 / 3) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                const wRadius = radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 15;
                waypoints.push({
                    x: Math.cos(wAngle) * wRadius,
                    z: Math.sin(wAngle) * wRadius
                });
            }
            
            group.userData = {
                ...data,
                waypoints,
                currentWaypoint: 0,
                speed: 0.04 + Math.random() * 0.025,
                hoverOffset: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
                shell,
                core,
                ring,
                id: i,
                emotionalState: 'neutral'
            };
            
            scene.add(group);
            entities.push(group);
        });
